# META
~~~ini
description=Cross-document reference values preserve lexical binding while missing and ambiguous operands and type hierarchies cannot create false type mismatches
type=multi
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
## values.sysml
~~~sysml
package Values {
    item def Value;
    item def Specialized :> Value;
    item source : Specialized;
    item def Holder { ref item value : Value; }
}
package First { item collision : Values::Value; }
package Second { item collision : Values::Value; }
~~~
## bindings.sysml
~~~sysml
package Bindings {
    private import Values::*;
    private import First::*;
    private import Second::*;
    item valid : Holder { ref item :>> value = source; }
    item ambiguous : Holder { ref item :>> value = collision; }
    item missing : Holder { ref item :>> value = unknown; }
    item def UnknownSubtype :> UnknownBase;
    item unsettled : UnknownSubtype;
    item deferred : Holder { ref item :>> value = unsettled; }
    requirement def Required { subject target : Value; }
    requirement requirement1 : Required;
    satisfy requirement1 by unknown;
    satisfy requirement1 by collision;
    satisfy requirement1 by unsettled;
}
~~~
